1 TX Audit Report

Source: experiments/transactions/tx-09edab2b/TX-AUDIT-REPORT.md · 16604 byte · SHA256: ac913a7c32958b5eff72e4e746ff1102338867ed007e31af374c7fccb84d7bd7

TX Audit Report — Rayls Enygma Week 2 Experiment

Transaction: 0x09edab2bb8914aea3c905608ea9fcd09c056a470a5e150df03e0b324eff525bb Network: Rayls Public Testnet, chain ID 7295799 (0x6f5337) Block: 24825928 (2026-11-21 09:40:42 UTC; exactly 1 transaction in the block) Audit date: 21–22 Nov 2026 (retrieved 21 Nov 16:13–16:29 UTC; Anara asleep, overnight job) Experiment: private payment of 6 RLS from @anara to @kittynim, recorded by Anara on video with ElevenLabs (Zara) voice-over. This audit examines the on-chain transaction only; the wallet UI / VO / video are treated as experiment context, not as on-chain evidence.

Labels used (same 6-label taxonomy as the initial audit): verified-fact / official-claim / direct-observation / reconstruction / inference / unknown. Every claim in decoded/audit_summary.json carries one of these labels.

Scripts (versions recorded; see manifest):

scriptversionrole
scripts/tx_audit_09edab2b.py1.1.0fetch raw data via curl (v1.0.0 used urllib and FAILED on transport — see §6)
scripts/decode_tx_09edab2b.py1.0.0decode + hex search + fee + summary (no network; reads raw files only)
scripts/verify_tx_audit_09edab2b.py1.0.0INDEPENDENT verification: re-reads evidence, recomputes SHA-256, checks manifest + cross-consistency

Evidence package: experiments/transactions/tx-09edab2b/ — raw JSON (immutable, chmod 444), decoded JSON, this report, gaps.md, TODO.md, manifest.json. SHA-256 for every file is in manifest.json. The combined review PDF is a derivative copy only (01_tx_audit_complete_history.pdf).

1. What was checked

  • RPC (curl, browser UA, up to 5 attempts per call): eth_chainId,

eth_getTransactionByHash, eth_getTransactionReceipt, eth_getBlockByNumber, eth_getLogs (single-block cross-check), eth_getCode + EIP-1967 slot reads for the destination/implementation/token/Multicall3/registry.

  • Explorer API: gettxinfo, gettxreceiptstatus, txlist for the relayer

(blocks 24825800–24826200), getLogs for UserRegistry UsernameSet (blocks 23212003–24910000, 200k-block pages).

  • Function selector + event topic0s: 4byte.directory (all count 0 — see §6),

then local pure-Python keccak256 against the demo-bundle IEnygmaDvp ABI (decoded/bundle-abi-extract.json; bundle SHA-256 7530bbc2… matches the audit §C.4 — bundle unchanged since 21 Nov).

  • Calldata parsed structurally as ABI words; logs decoded per the bundle ABI;

case-insensitive hex search for sender/receiver/token/relayer/amounts; fee computed from receipt values; one related relayer tx fetched and decoded.

2. Findings per question

Q1 — Receipt / status — verified-fact

Status 0x1 (success). Block 24825928, transactionIndex 0, gasUsed 1,316,001 of gasLimit 1,630,484, effectiveGasPrice 48,000,000,000 wei, 7 logs, contractAddress null. Block 24825928 contains exactly this one transaction. Explorer agrees on block, from, gas limit/price/used, status, hash.

Q2 — Calldata / function — verified-fact (with a stated limit)

Function is transferV2. Selector 0xaacd552c equals the first 4 bytes of keccak256 of the canonical signature transferV2((((uint256,uint256),(uint256[2],uint256[2]),(uint256,uint256)),uint256[],uint256,uint256),bytes[],bytes[]) from the bundle ABI. Calldata (4,804 bytes) parses cleanly with zero trailing bytes: receipt tuple with an 8-word (256-byte) Groth16 proof — matching the VO claim of a 256-byte proof — statement of 10 uint256 values, numberOfInputs 1, numberOfOutputs 3, ciphertextI 3 entries × 1,088 bytes, ciphertextII 3 entries × 92 bytes. Limit: no verified contract source exists for the deployed vault implementation, so what transferV2 does internally is NOT proven from source; the function identity and the calldata structure are proven from the selector match + the clean structural parse. The ABI used is the demo app's client-side ABI, not verified on-chain source.

Q3 — Destination contract / SharedVault — verified-fact

Destination is the SharedVaultProxy (yx) 0x9C5Ad5e5Ea5b5910E7fD1E1CC7b70ce0e4bF06c2, called directly (NOT via Multicall3 — unlike the audit's sampled shielded-transfer tx 0x7f72…). Its EIP-1967 implementation slot reads 0xAd9667049f14D1757D147F652c41Dc241C57091C, matching the live vault-yx implementation recorded in the audit (§C.3). The Explorer's displayed method name (transferV2) is a cross-check only; the proof is the selector match (Q2).

Q4 — Event logs — verified-fact

7 logs, all from the vault proxy: 1× Nullifier (vaultId 2, treeNumber 0, nullifier 0x0c0f23a6…), 3× Commitment (vaultId 2, commitments 0x094b233a…, 0x2304db2e…, 0x206ed94a…), 3× EncryptedNote (same 3 commitments, each with 1,312 bytes of ciphertext data = two byte arrays: ciphertextI 1,088 bytes + ciphertextII 92 bytes). No ERC20 Transfer log, no PaidOut, no PartialWithdrawal, no log from any other address. This matches the audit's shielded-transfer classification (Commitment + EncryptedNote + Nullifier, no payout). The 3 calldata ciphertexts are byte-identical (SHA-256) to the 3 log ciphertexts.

Q5 — Commitment / encrypted-note observability — direct-observation

Commitment hashes are public (3 hashes in topics and in the calldata statement). EncryptedNote ciphertexts are public as opaque bytes (1,312 bytes each; lengths + SHA-256 fingerprints recorded). The note VALUES (amounts, sender, receiver, token) are NOT visible in the ciphertext. One input note was spent (1 nullifier) and 3 output notes were created (3 commitments).

Q6 — Hidden vs visible — direct-observation

NOT visible anywhere in the raw transaction input, receipt, or logs (case-insensitive hex search, bare address and 32-byte-word forms): receiver EVM 0x680d…0efc, sender EVM 0x0c5b…0b5, RLS token proxy 0x07e1…7ea, amount 6 RLS as 6e18, fee values 3.84 / 0.58 / 4.42 RLS as 18-decimal words. FOUND in the on-chain data: the fee TOTAL 4.416 RLS as a 32-byte word in the calldata (statement[8] = 4,416,000,000,000,000,000 wei); the relayer address as tx.from; the vault proxy address (also as statement[7]); the spent nullifier hash and the 3 commitment hashes (in the calldata statement and in log topics). The 6 RLS figure, the two usernames, and the token type come only from the wallet UI / VO / video (direct observation of the experiment), NOT from on-chain data. "Not visible in this data" is NOT proof that the information is unknowable in principle: parties holding the relevant keys (receiver view key, sender keys, auditor keys) could decrypt the notes; this audit does not test that.

Statement layout (10 values): [0]=0, [1]=0, [2]=0x0d6edc87… (unidentified hash — position before the nullifier is consistent with a Merkle root, not proven), [3]=nullifier (verified vs log 0), [4–6]=the 3 commitments (verified vs logs 1–3), [7]=vault address (verified vs to-address — the proof is bound to this vault), [8]=4.416 RLS fee (verified), [9]=0x0f58ff41… (unidentified hash — position after the fee is consistent with a binding/ciphertext hash, not proven).

Q7 — Relayer role / sender link — reconstruction

tx.from is the relayer 0xa5f0c684eb49866a9f62f901cd85f98a19da0ac8 (verified-fact). The registry shows that address holds the username rayls-relayer (verified-fact from UsernameSet logs; first registration in the set). The docs claim the relayer submits user transactions and the public sender shown is the relayer (official-claim). Whether THIS tx was submitted on behalf of @anara (0x0c5b…0b5) is a reconstruction, not an on-chain fact: the on-chain data contains no link to @anara (no sender address, no username, no key). The link rests on experiment context: the same block/time/method/vault as the success screen in the recorded experiment, the matching nullifier/commitment pattern, and the shielded-balance decrease shown in the wallet UI. from=relayer alone is NOT proof that the relayer is the sender, and the absence of the sender address is NOT proof of absolute unlinkability. Registry resolutions (verified-fact): @anara → 0x0c5b369ad7d0c4e051a2887575f7fc85e0ccd0b5 (block 0x1645a5f); @kittynim → 0x680d90983da58bcb7d127e3421193274e84e0efc (block 0x167bc88, matches the address shown in the video). 195 usernames observed (audit §D.6 recorded 194 at 21 Nov; +1 since).

Q8 — Fee vs wallet — verified-fact (fee total), reconstruction (split)

  • On-chain gas for the target tx: 1,316,001 × 48,000,000,000 wei =

63,168,048,000,000,000 wei = 0.063168048 RLS (18 decimals assumed). This does NOT equal the wallet-displayed "gas" fee of 3.84 RLS. The wallet's "gas" label is the relayer service fee, not the chain gas cost.

  • The TOTAL fee IS public on-chain: calldata statement[8] = 4.416 RLS

exactly, matching the wallet detail-activity fee 4.416 RLS.

  • The 0.58 foundation fee has an on-chain counterpart: 4 blocks later (block

24825932) the relayer transferred 0.576 RLS of the RLS token to 0x71af09966cbed4434cca97c40fa38b8083548b95 (tx 0xacc7d175…, verified-fact; 0.576 rounds to 0.58 at 2 decimals). That address is recorded in the audit map as the Auditor EOA; whether it is the Rayls Foundation fee address is UNKNOWN.

  • The split closes exactly: 4.416 = 3.84 + 0.576, and **3.84 × 15% =

0.576 exactly**. So the docs' "relayer fee 15% to Rayls Foundation" reads as 15% OF THE RELAYER FEE (not of the payment): the foundation portion (0.576) is verified on-chain, the relayer-retained portion (3.84) is determined arithmetically from two verified on-chain numbers, though it never appears as its own word in the data.

  • Reconstruction (not verified): 1 input note spent, 3 output notes created;

the sender shielded balance fell by 10.416 RLS = 6 payment + 4.416 fee, so the 4.416 fee plausibly sits inside the shielded notes (one output note to the relayer, of which 0.576 went on to the foundation address and 3.84 stayed with the relayer). Note values are encrypted, so this mapping cannot be verified from the chain.

4. Relation to the prior archives

Strengthens:

  • Audit §D.7 shielded-transfer classification (Commitment + EncryptedNote +

Nullifier, no payout) — this tx is a clean instance: 1/3/3.

  • Audit §F privacy limits — sender, receiver, amount, token type are absent

from the tx data, as the docs claim; the audit's "shielded transfers reveal no who/how much/which token" holds for this tx.

  • Audit §C.3 vault-yx implementation (0xAd966704…) — re-confirmed live via

the EIP-1967 slot.

  • Bundle hash 7530bbc2… unchanged since the 21 Nov audit; the bundle ABI

resolves the function and all three event topic0s on-chain.

Adds (new evidence):

  • The fee TOTAL (4.416 RLS) is a public statement value, not hidden — the

docs' "a shielded send shows only that a send happened, the relayer, and its gas fee" is imprecise: this tx publicly commits to the fee amount and to the vault address in the statement.

  • The foundation fee has a concrete on-chain settlement: 0.576 RLS to

0x71af… (Auditor EOA per the audit map) 4 blocks after the payment.

  • "Relayer fee 15% to Rayls Foundation" is consistent with 15% OF THE

RELAYER FEE (3.84 × 15% = 0.576), not 15% of the payment (6 × 15% = 0.9).

  • The wallet's "3.84 gas" is a relayer service fee, not chain gas (chain gas

is 0.063 RLS). The 4.42 vs 4.416 difference is display rounding (4.416 rounds to 4.42), not a discrepancy.

  • Registry resolutions for both experiment accounts (@anara, @kittynim).
  • Statement layout for transferV2 (positions 3–8 verified by value matching;

positions 0, 1, 2, 9 unidentified — a new gap).

Contradicts nothing in the prior archives. No prior finding was overturned. The 0.0001 RLS fee discrepancy from the earlier demo is a different item and stays UNRESOLVED (not addressed here).

5. Limitations

  1. No verified contract source for the deployed vault implementation; ABIs

come from the demo-bundle client code.

  1. 4byte.directory has no entry for the selector or the three event topic0s.
  2. Note values are encrypted; the 3-output-note mapping (6 / 3.908 / 4.416)

is a reconstruction, not a verified fact.

  1. The 3.84 component never appears as its own word; determined

arithmetically (4.416 − 0.576).

  1. Whether 0x71af… (Auditor EOA) is the Rayls Foundation fee address is

unknown.

  1. Statement positions 0, 1, 2, 9 are unidentified (no published layout).
  2. One transaction + one related tx only; nothing is proven about other

shielded transfers.

6. Method notes (honest record)

  • Fetch v1.0.0 failed on transport (urllib): eth_getTransactionByHash,

eth_getTransactionReceipt, eth_getLogs died with IncompleteRead / RemoteDisconnected (5/5 attempts each); small responses succeeded. No valid data was captured; the partial raw directory was removed. v1.1.0 switched the transport to curl and passed 18/18.

  • RPC eth_getLogs range limit: the node rejects ranges over 100,000

blocks (query exceeds max block range 100000, code −32602). The registry lookup fell back to Explorer API pagination (200k-block pages).

  • Decode bugs found and fixed during development: (a) the first parse read

ABI head offsets from the wrong base (input start instead of after the 4-byte selector), giving numberOfInputs/Outputs 0; (b) the bytes[] parse treated element offsets as lengths. Both were fixed, the derived outputs were deleted and regenerated from scratch; the final script (v1.0.0 on disk) parses the calldata with zero trailing bytes and SHA-256-matched ciphertexts. The raw files were never touched.

  • The related tx (0xacc7d175…) was found by listing the relayer's txs in

blocks 24825800–24826200 (exactly 2 txs: the target and the fee settlement). It is saved as raw/related-tx-0xacc7d175.json and labelled as a related tx, not part of the target tx.

Paths are repo-relative under experiments/transactions/tx-09edab2b/. Byte sizes are listed here; SHA-256 for every file is in manifest.json.

2 Remaining gaps

Source: experiments/transactions/tx-09edab2b/gaps.md · 5004 byte · SHA256: cc08501f09a3ff61e18c845db502c2958c1920b1590c45b2f5cce067e411460d

Remaining gaps — after the Week 2 TX audit (0x09edab2b…)

For each gap from the initial audit (ENYGMA-TESTNET-AUDIT-REPORT.md §H/§I and the experiment plan §G): what this TX audit resolves vs what stays unknown. Nothing here is forced to an answer.

1. Deployment identity unknowns — STAYS UNKNOWN

  • bx proxies do not match the bundle (live registry/vault bx implementations

are not in the bundle; the bx ownerCalls upgrade was never executed). This TX audit touches only the yx vault; the bx mismatch is untouched.

  • EnygmaDvp contract (0x7ec57a8f…) and forwarder rotation (new

forwarder 0x12B66400… already used by the latest registration): untouched.

  • RLS token proxy EIP-1967 slot anomaly (slot contains its own address):

untouched.

  • New sub-question from this audit: is 0x71af09966cbed4434cca97c40fa38b8083548b95

(Auditor EOA per the audit map) the Rayls Foundation fee address? The 0.576 RLS settlement went there, but no source links the two roles.

2. Selective auditing — UNVERIFIED (unchanged)

  • Still 7 AuditorAdded / 4 AuditorKeyGiven / 1 AuditorPersonalKeySet (10 Nov);

this TX audit adds no auditor evidence. The auditor-access scope conflict (AMA/blog: scoped; docs/reference repo: network-wide) is still unresolved.

  • New observation, NOT verification: the Auditor EOA received the 0.576 RLS

foundation-fee settlement. Receiving a fee does not prove auditor access to shielded data, and must not be cited as such.

  • Ticket-first before any public conclusion (mod guidance).

3. Shielded-transfer observability limits — PARTIALLY RESOLVED

  • Resolved for one transfer: this tx proves the pattern end-to-end —

1 nullifier spent, 3 commitments + 3 encrypted notes created, statement with vault binding + public fee total (4.416 RLS), no plaintext sender / receiver / amount / token in the tx data. The docs' "shows only that a send happened, the relayer, and its gas fee" is imprecise for this tx (the fee total and the vault address are also public in the statement; the "gas fee" shown is the relayer fee, not chain gas).

  • Stays unknown: whether shielding amounts are visible via ERC-20

Transfer logs (audit §G.2) — this tx is a shielded transfer and has NO Transfer log, but the shielding path itself was not tested here. The relayer-from check across all 511 vault txs (audit §I) was not done — only this tx + the related fee tx were checked.

  • New gap: transferV2 statement positions 0, 1, 2, 9 are unidentified

(two zeros, two hashes). Positions 3–8 are verified by value matching; the Merkle-root / binding-hash readings for 2 and 9 are inferences only.

4. Docs / contracts unproven — PARTIALLY RESOLVED

  • Resolved: the bundle ABI's transferV2 signature is confirmed on-chain

(selector 0xaacd552c = keccak256 of the canonical signature); the three event signatures are confirmed on-chain (topic0 matches); the bundle hash is unchanged since 21 Nov.

  • Stays unproven: no verified contract source for the deployed vault

implementation — transferV2's internal logic (proof verification, note handling, fee accounting) is not proven from source. The "relayer fee 15% to Rayls Foundation" claim is now consistent with 15% OF THE RELAYER FEE (3.84 × 15% = 0.576 exactly), but the docs do not state the base explicitly — confirm with the team before publishing that reading.

5. Fee discrepancies — PARTIALLY RESOLVED

  • Resolved for this experiment: 4.42 vs 4.416 = display rounding

(statement[8] = 4.416 RLS exactly); the wallet's 3.84 "gas" is the relayer service fee (4.416 − 0.576 = 3.84), not chain gas (0.063168048 RLS); the 0.58 foundation fee = 0.576 RLS on-chain (rounds to 0.58).

  • Stays UNRESOLVED: the 0.0001 RLS fee discrepancy from the earlier demo

(a different experiment) — never call it rounding; untouched by this audit.

  • Stays unknown: why the relayer fee is 3.84 RLS for this payment

(flat fee? computed from what?); whether the fee-settlement pattern (a separate ERC20 transfer a few blocks later) holds for other shielded transfers.

6. Proof time — UNVERIFIED (unchanged)

  • The VO's "256-byte proof, generated in 10 seconds" — the 256-byte size is

confirmed on-chain (proof struct = 8 words = 256 bytes); the 10-second generation time is not measurable on-chain and was not re-measured here. Proof-time figures still differ across sources (408 ms vs ~2.2 s vs ~3 s vs "under 4 s").

7. Other audit §I items — NOT DONE (still open)

  • Experiments G.1–G.6 (need Anara's approval): shielding ERC-20 visibility,

fee experiments, relayer from sweep, proof-time measurement.

  • Implementation source verification vs the public repo.
  • Continuous monitoring + separate finality experiment; load test only in an

appropriate environment.

  • Read IACR ePrint 2025/1638 + 2025/1639; re-check demo persistence; verify

EnygmaDvp; check USDr precompile nature.
